<!-- ===== FILE: docs/handoff/reusable-code/hadithGrade.ts ===== -->
/**
 * Which hadith may enter the app.
 *
 * Current scope (00-decisions.md §5): al-Bukhari and Muslim only. Every other
 * collection is deferred, whatever its grading. `decide()` applies the scope.
 *
 * Kept for when the scope widens: option B, applied to the gradings
 * sunnah.com records for each hadith (as copied by scripts/fetch-hadith.ts
 * from the open hadith-api dataset):
 *
 * - In al-Bukhari or Muslim: include.
 * - Otherwise al-Albani's grading decides, unless one of the dissent graders
 *   (Shu'ayb al-Arna'ut, Ahmad Shakir, Bashar Awad Ma'ruf) grades it weak:
 *   then the scholarly reviewer decides.
 * - No grading from al-Albani, a grading that is not a prophetic attribution
 *   (mawquf, maqtu', mursal), or a wording this module does not know: the
 *   reviewer decides.
 *
 * This module only reads grade labels. It contains no hadith text.
 */

export type GradeClass = 'accepted' | 'weak' | 'not_marfu' | 'unknown';

export interface Grading {
  /** Grader as sunnah.com names them, e.g. "Al-Albani". */
  by: string;
  /** Grade as sunnah.com writes it, e.g. "Sahih", "Isnaad Hasan", "Daif". */
  grade: string;
}

export type DecisionKind = 'include' | 'review' | 'exclude' | 'defer';

/** 'sahihayn': al-Bukhari and Muslim only (current). 'ruleB': option B over the sunan as well. */
export type Scope = 'sahihayn' | 'ruleB';
export const CURRENT_SCOPE: Scope = 'sahihayn';

export interface Decision {
  decision: DecisionKind;
  /** Why, in Arabic, for the verification report. */
  reason: string;
}

export const SAHIHAYN = new Set(['bukhari', 'muslim']);
export const PRIMARY_GRADER = 'Al-Albani';
export const DISSENT_GRADERS = ['Shuaib Al Arnaut', 'Ahmad Muhammad Shakir', 'Bashar Awad Maarouf'];

export const GRADER_NAMES_AR: Record<string, string> = {
  'Al-Albani': 'الألباني',
  'Shuaib Al Arnaut': 'شعيب الأرنؤوط',
  'Ahmad Muhammad Shakir': 'أحمد شاكر',
  'Bashar Awad Maarouf': 'بشار عواد معروف',
  'Zubair Ali Zai': 'زبير علي زئي',
  'Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid': 'محمد محيي الدين عبد الحميد',
  'Muhammad Fouad Abd al-Baqi': 'محمد فؤاد عبد الباقي',
  'Abu Ghuddah': 'عبد الفتاح أبو غدة',
};

const WEAK = /\b(daif|da'if|mawdu|maudu|munkar|shadh|batil)\b/i;
const NOT_MARFU = /\b(muquf|mauquf|mawquf|maqtu|mursal)\b/i;
const ACCEPTED = /\b(sahih|hasan)\b/i;

/** Sorts one grade label. Weak wins over anything else in the same label. */
export function classifyGrade(grade: string): GradeClass {
  const g = grade.trim();
  if (WEAK.test(g)) return 'weak';
  if (NOT_MARFU.test(g)) return 'not_marfu';
  if (ACCEPTED.test(g)) return 'accepted';
  return 'unknown';
}

const LABELS_AR: Array<[RegExp, string]> = [
  [/^hasan sahih$/i, 'حسن صحيح'],
  [/^(isnaad sahih|sahih isnaad)$/i, 'إسناده صحيح'],
  [/^(isnaad hasan|hasan isnaad)$/i, 'إسناده حسن'],
  [/^sahih lighairihi$/i, 'صحيح لغيره'],
  [/^hasan lighairihi$/i, 'حسن لغيره'],
  [/^sahih$/i, 'صحيح'],
  [/^hasan$/i, 'حسن'],
];

/** Arabic label for the common accepted grades, or null when a person should word it. */
export function gradeLabelAr(grade: string): string | null {
  const g = grade.trim();
  for (const [pattern, label] of LABELS_AR) if (pattern.test(g)) return label;
  return null;
}

export function decideByRuleB(collection: string, gradings: readonly Grading[]): Decision {
  if (SAHIHAYN.has(collection)) {
    return { decision: 'include', reason: 'في الصحيحين' };
  }
  const primary = gradings.find((g) => g.by === PRIMARY_GRADER);
  if (!primary) {
    return { decision: 'review', reason: 'لا حكم للألباني في المصدر' };
  }
  const primaryClass = classifyGrade(primary.grade);
  if (primaryClass === 'weak') {
    return { decision: 'exclude', reason: 'ضعّفه الألباني' };
  }
  if (primaryClass !== 'accepted') {
    return { decision: 'review', reason: `حكم الألباني يحتاج نظرًا: ${primary.grade}` };
  }
  const dissent = gradings.filter((g) => DISSENT_GRADERS.includes(g.by) && classifyGrade(g.grade) === 'weak');
  if (dissent.length > 0) {
    const who = dissent.map((g) => GRADER_NAMES_AR[g.by] ?? g.by).join('، ');
    return { decision: 'review', reason: `صححه الألباني وضعّفه ${who}` };
  }
  const notMarfu = gradings.some((g) => DISSENT_GRADERS.includes(g.by) && classifyGrade(g.grade) === 'not_marfu');
  if (notMarfu) {
    return { decision: 'review', reason: 'أحد المحققين جعله موقوفًا أو مقطوعًا أو مرسلًا' };
  }
  return { decision: 'include', reason: 'صححه أو حسّنه الألباني، ولم يضعّفه أحد من المحققين المعتبرين في القاعدة' };
}

/** The decision under the current scope, or under the scope given. */
export function decide(collection: string, gradings: readonly Grading[], scope: Scope = CURRENT_SCOPE): Decision {
  if (scope === 'sahihayn') {
    return SAHIHAYN.has(collection)
      ? { decision: 'include', reason: 'في الصحيحين' }
      : { decision: 'defer', reason: 'خارج الصحيحين: مؤجل بقرار صاحب المشروع' };
  }
  return decideByRuleB(collection, gradings);
}
<!-- ===== END FILE ===== -->

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/handoff/reusable-code/hijri.ts ===== -->
import { toArabicNumerals } from './arabic';
import { toLocalDateString } from './dates';

/**
 * Native Arabic Hijri date formatter using Islamic calendar
 */
export function getFormattedHijriDate(date: Date = new Date()): string {
  const normalizeHijriSuffix = (formatted: string) => {
    // Strip any trailing هـ or ه that Intl.DateTimeFormat may already include, then append a single هـ
    const cleaned = formatted.replace(/\s*هـ?\s*$/u, '').trim();
    return `${cleaned} هـ`;
  };

  try {
    const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-SA-u-ca-islamic-umalqura', {
      day: 'numeric',
      month: 'long',
      year: 'numeric'
    });
    return normalizeHijriSuffix(formatter.format(date));
  } catch {
    // Fallback if umalqura subtag not supported
    try {
      const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-SA-u-ca-islamic', {
        day: 'numeric',
        month: 'long',
        year: 'numeric'
      });
      return normalizeHijriSuffix(formatter.format(date));
    } catch {
      return 'اليوم المبارك';
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Returns weekday + Hijri day + month, e.g. "الأحد ٢٥ ذو الحجة"
 */
export function getFormattedHijriDayHeader(date: Date = new Date()): string {
  try {
    const weekdayFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-SA', { weekday: 'long' });
    const weekday = weekdayFormatter.format(date);

    const dayMonthFormatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-SA-u-ca-islamic-umalqura', {
      day: 'numeric',
      month: 'long'
    });
    return `${weekday} ${dayMonthFormatter.format(date)}`;
  } catch {
    return 'اليوم';
  }
}

/**
 * Returns Hijri month and year e.g. "ذو الحجة ١٤٤٧"
 */
export function getHijriMonthAndYear(date: Date = new Date()): { monthName: string; year: string; full: string } {
  try {
    const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-SA-u-ca-islamic-umalqura', {
      month: 'long',
      year: 'numeric'
    }).formatToParts(date);

    const month = parts.find(p => p.type === 'month')?.value || 'ذو الحجة';
    const year = parts.find(p => p.type === 'year')?.value || '١٤٤٧';

    return {
      monthName: month,
      year: year,
      full: `${month} ${year}`
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      monthName: 'ذو الحجة',
      year: '١٤٤٧',
      full: 'ذو الحجة ١٤٤٧'
    };
  }
}

export interface HijriCalendarDay {
  dayNumber: number; // 1 to 29 or 30
  arabicNumber: string;
  isoDate: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  gregorianLabel: string; // e.g., "٢٠ أكتوبر ٢٠٢٦"
  hijriFullLabel: string; // e.g., "٩ جمادى الأولى ١٤٤٨ هـ"
  isToday: boolean;
  isFuture: boolean;
  weekdayIndex: number; // 0=Saturday (س), 1=Sunday (ح), 2=Monday (ن), 3=Tuesday (ث), 4=Wednesday (ر), 5=Thursday (خ), 6=Friday (ج)
}

function parseArabicInt(str: string): number {
  const normalized = str.replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (d) => String('٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩'.indexOf(d))).replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
  return parseInt(normalized, 10) || 1;
}

function getHijriNumericParts(date: Date): { day: number; monthName: string; year: string } {
  try {
    const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-SA-u-ca-islamic-umalqura', {
      day: 'numeric',
      month: 'long',
      year: 'numeric'
    }).formatToParts(date);

    const dayStr = parts.find(p => p.type === 'day')?.value || '1';
    const monthName = parts.find(p => p.type === 'month')?.value || 'ذو الحجة';
    const year = parts.find(p => p.type === 'year')?.value || '١٤٤٧';

    return {
      day: parseArabicInt(dayStr),
      monthName,
      year
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      day: date.getDate(),
      monthName: 'ذو الحجة',
      year: '١٤٤٧'
    };
  }
}

function formatGregorianArabic(date: Date): string {
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-SA-u-ca-gregory', {
      day: 'numeric',
      month: 'long',
      year: 'numeric'
    }).format(date);
  } catch {
    return toLocalDateString(date);
  }
}

/**
 * Computes exact Hijri calendar days for the target Hijri month (supports monthOffset navigation)
 */
export function generateMonthCalendar(referenceDate: Date = new Date(), monthOffset: number = 0): {
  monthTitle: string;
  monthName: string;
  year: string;
  days: HijriCalendarDay[];
  startPadding: number; // Empty slots before the 1st day (Saturday-based)
} {
  // Normalize to noon to avoid DST / timezone midnight boundary shifts
  const cursor = new Date(
    referenceDate.getFullYear(),
    referenceDate.getMonth(),
    referenceDate.getDate(),
    12,
    0,
    0
  );

  // Step 1: Move cursor to the 1st of the current Hijri month
  const currentHijri = getHijriNumericParts(cursor);
  cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - (currentHijri.day - 1));

  // Verify and fine-tune to exact day 1 of current Hijri month
  while (getHijriNumericParts(cursor).day > 1) {
    cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
  }

  // Step 2: Apply Hijri monthOffset accurately month-by-month
  if (monthOffset !== 0) {
    const direction = monthOffset > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    let remaining = Math.abs(monthOffset);

    while (remaining > 0) {
      if (direction > 0) {
        // Jump to next Hijri month (28 days forward is still day 29 or 1, then step until day === 1)
        cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() + 28);
        while (getHijriNumericParts(cursor).day !== 1) {
          cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() + 1);
        }
      } else {
        // Step back 1 day into the previous Hijri month, then rewind to day 1 of that month
        cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
        const prevMonthParts = getHijriNumericParts(cursor);
        cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - (prevMonthParts.day - 1));
        while (getHijriNumericParts(cursor).day > 1) {
          cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() - 1);
        }
      }
      remaining--;
    }
  }

  const targetMonthParts = getHijriNumericParts(cursor);
  const todayNoon = new Date();
  todayNoon.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const todayIso = toLocalDateString();

  const days: HijriCalendarDay[] = [];
  const dayCursor = new Date(cursor);

  // Collect all days belonging to this Hijri month (29 or 30 days)
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
    const parts = getHijriNumericParts(dayCursor);
    if (i > 0 && parts.day === 1) {
      break; // Reached the 1st of the next Hijri month
    }

    const yyyy = dayCursor.getFullYear();
    const mm = String(dayCursor.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const dd = String(dayCursor.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    const isoDate = `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;

    const jsDay = dayCursor.getDay();
    const satFirstIndex = (jsDay + 1) % 7;
    const dayNum = parts.day || (i + 1);

    days.push({
      dayNumber: dayNum,
      arabicNumber: toArabicNumerals(dayNum),
      isoDate,
      gregorianLabel: formatGregorianArabic(dayCursor),
      hijriFullLabel: `${toArabicNumerals(dayNum)} ${targetMonthParts.monthName} ${targetMonthParts.year} هـ`,
      isToday: isoDate === todayIso,
      isFuture: dayCursor.getTime() > todayNoon.getTime() && isoDate !== todayIso,
      weekdayIndex: satFirstIndex
    });

    dayCursor.setDate(dayCursor.getDate() + 1);
  }

  const firstDayWeekday = days.length > 0 ? days[0].weekdayIndex : 0;

  return {
    monthTitle: `${targetMonthParts.monthName} ${targetMonthParts.year} هـ`,
    monthName: targetMonthParts.monthName,
    year: targetMonthParts.year,
    days,
    startPadding: firstDayWeekday
  };
}

<!-- ===== END FILE ===== -->

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/handoff/reusable-code/useDialog.ts ===== -->
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

const FOCUSABLE = [
  'a[href]',
  'button:not([disabled])',
  'input:not([disabled])',
  'select:not([disabled])',
  'textarea:not([disabled])',
  '[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
].join(',');

interface UseDialogOptions {
  /** Called when the user presses Escape. */
  onClose?: () => void;
  /** Set to false while the dialog is not shown, or while another dialog on top of it should own the keyboard. */
  active?: boolean;
  /** Change this when the dialog's content is replaced (for example a new step), to move focus back to the dialog. */
  focusKey?: string | number;
}

/**
 * Keyboard and focus behaviour for a modal dialog: focus moves into the dialog
 * when it opens, Tab stays inside it, Escape closes it, the page behind does
 * not scroll, and focus returns to where it was when the dialog closes.
 *
 * Attach the returned ref to the dialog's root element, which needs
 * `tabIndex={-1}`, `role="dialog"` and `aria-modal="true"`.
 */
export function useDialog<T extends HTMLElement>({ onClose, active = true, focusKey }: UseDialogOptions = {}) {
  const ref = useRef<T | null>(null);
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose);
  onCloseRef.current = onClose;

  // Keyboard handling, scroll lock, and returning focus on close.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;

    const previouslyFocused = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const root = ref.current;
      if (!root) return;

      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        if (onCloseRef.current) {
          event.stopPropagation();
          onCloseRef.current();
        }
        return;
      }

      if (event.key !== 'Tab') return;
      const focusable = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE)).filter(
        (el) => el.offsetParent !== null || el === document.activeElement
      );
      if (focusable.length === 0) {
        event.preventDefault();
        root.focus();
        return;
      }
      const first = focusable[0];
      const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
      const current = document.activeElement;
      if (!root.contains(current)) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      } else if (event.shiftKey && (current === first || current === root)) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && current === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      if (previouslyFocused && document.contains(previouslyFocused)) {
        previouslyFocused.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      }
    };
  }, [active]);

  // Move focus into the dialog when it opens and whenever its content is replaced.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;
    const root = ref.current;
    if (root && !root.contains(document.activeElement)) {
      root.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
  }, [active, focusKey]);

  return ref;
}
<!-- ===== END FILE ===== -->

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/handoff/tools/build_catalogue_texts.py ===== -->
"""Copies the source text of every reference in the seed catalogue into a
Markdown file, verbatim, for the researcher and the reviewer.

Nothing here is typed: the Qur'an text is read from Tanzil's Uthmani file and
each hadith from the open hadith-api dataset, by reference, and written out
unchanged. A character the source itself has corrupted (U+FFFD) is flagged,
never repaired by hand.

Usage: python3 -I build_catalogue_texts.py DOWNLOADS_DIR > ../04c-catalogue-texts.md
Only al-Bukhari and Muslim are copied (the current scope); pass --all for every collection.
DOWNLOADS_DIR needs quran/uthmani.txt (Tanzil, txt-2, Uthmani) and
hadith/<collection>.json (hadith-api editions/ara-<collection>.min.json).
"""
import json, os, sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from catalogue_refs import Q, S  # noqa: E402

D = sys.argv[1]
quran, notice = {}, []
for line in open(f'{D}/quran/uthmani.txt', encoding='utf-8'):
    parts = line.rstrip('\n').split('|')
    if len(parts) == 3 and parts[0].isdigit():
        quran[(int(parts[0]), int(parts[1]))] = parts[2]
    elif line.startswith('#'):
        notice.append(line.rstrip('\n'))

H = {c: json.load(open(f'{D}/hadith/{c}.json'))['hadiths']
     for c in ['bukhari', 'muslim', 'abudawud', 'tirmidhi', 'nasai', 'ibnmajah']}
CN = {'bukhari': 'صحيح البخاري', 'muslim': 'صحيح مسلم', 'abudawud': 'سنن أبي داود', 'tirmidhi': 'جامع الترمذي',
      'nasai': 'سنن النسائي', 'ibnmajah': 'سنن ابن ماجه'}

def find(coll, num):
    if coll == 'muslim':
        return [h for h in H[coll] if str(h.get('arabicnumber', '')).split('.')[0] == str(num)]
    return [h for h in H[coll] if h.get('hadithnumber') == num]

out = []
w = out.append
w('# ٤ج. نصوص القائمة المرشّحة من مصادرها\n')
w('هذا الملف **مولَّد بالسكريبت** [tools/build_catalogue_texts.py](tools/build_catalogue_texts.py): كل نص فيه منسوخ حرفيًا من مصدره بالمرجع، ولم يُكتب منه حرف يدويًا. لا يُعدَّل هذا الملف باليد؛ يُعاد توليده.\n')
w('| | المصدر | الحالة |\n|---|---|---|')
w('| القرآن | Tanzil، النص العثماني (Uthmani 1.1)، الآيات كاملة | نص موثّق من مصدره. **المقطع الذي هو الدعاء** داخل الآية يحدده المراجع عند بناء البطاقة |')
w('| السنة | مجموعة البيانات المفتوحة `fawazahmed0/hadith-api` (مأخوذة من sunnah.com)، الحديث كاملًا بإسناده | **مصدر واحد، للمراجعة لا للنشر.** يُطابَق على مصدر ثانٍ (الدرر السنية أو الطبعة المطبوعة) قبل أن يُنقل إلى بطاقة |\n')
w('المراجع وأرقام الصفوف كما في [04-seed-catalogue.md](04-seed-catalogue.md). **من السنة: البخاري ومسلم فقط**، وهو النطاق الحالي بقرار صاحب المشروع؛ أحاديث الكتب الأخرى مؤجلة ولا تُنسخ هنا.\n')

w('## أ. القرآن\n')
for row, s, a, b in Q:
    ref = f'{s}:{a}' + (f'–{b}' if b != a else '')
    w(f'### {row} · {ref}\n')
    for i in range(a, b + 1):
        w(f'> {quran[(s, i)]} ({s}:{i})\n')

w('## ب. السنة\n')
ALL = '--all' in sys.argv[2:]
for row, refs in S:
    for coll, num in refs:
        if num is None or (not ALL and coll not in ('bukhari', 'muslim')):
            continue
        for h in find(coll, num):
            n = h.get('arabicnumber') if coll == 'muslim' else h.get('hadithnumber')
            w(f'### {row} · {CN[coll]} {n}\n')
            text = h['text']
            if '�' in text:
                w('> ⚠ **في هذا النص حرف تالف في المصدر نفسه (يظهر كرمز غير مقروء). لا يُصلَح باليد: يُنقل من مصدر ثانٍ.**\n')
            w(f'> {text}\n')

w('## ترخيص نص القرآن\n')
w('```')
w('\n'.join(l.lstrip('#').rstrip() for l in notice if l.strip('#').strip() and 'PLEASE DO NOT' not in l and not set(l.strip('#').strip()) <= set('=')))
w('```')
print('\n'.join(out))
<!-- ===== END FILE ===== -->

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/handoff/tools/build_single_file.py ===== -->
"""Packs the whole kit into one Markdown file to hand to Claude in a new session.

Each file is written verbatim between two marker lines, so the new session can
split the pack back into the same files (the header of the pack carries the
split script). Left out: 04c-catalogue-texts.md, which the new session
regenerates from the sources, and the PNG in assets/.

Usage: python3 -I build_single_file.py ../../qareeb-kit.md   (the pack lives next to the kit folder)
Regenerate the pack after any change to the kit.
"""
import pathlib, sys

KIT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
OPEN = '<!-- ' + '===== FILE: {} =====' + ' -->'
CLOSE = '<!-- ' + '===== END FILE =====' + ' -->'

ORDER = [
    'README.md', '00-decisions.md', '01-product-brief.md', '02-ux-and-screens.md', '03-content-model.md',
    '04-seed-catalogue.md', '04b-catalogue-verification.md', '05-technical-blueprint.md',
    '06-lessons-from-alhamd.md', '07-roadmap-and-launch.md', '08-kickoff-prompt.md',
    '09-content-drafting-prompt.md', '10-identity-brief.md',
]
SKIP = {'04c-catalogue-texts.md'}

HEADER = '''# «قريب»: حقيبة البدء في ملف واحد

> **إلى Claude:** هذا الملف يحمل حقيبة بدء مشروع «قريب» كاملة، مولَّدة من المجلد `handoff/dua-app` في المستودع `adel3li/Alhamd-design-v2`. اتبع الخطوات بالترتيب، ولا تعدّل محتوى الملفات وأنت تفكّها.

## الخطوة ١: فكّ الملف إلى ملفاته

كل ملف في هذه الحزمة محصور بين سطرين: سطر يبدأ بـ `<!-- ===== FILE:` ويذكر مسار الملف في المستودع، وسطر `END FILE`. ما بينهما هو الملف حرفيًا.

احفظ هذا الملف في المستودع الجديد باسم `docs/handoff/QAREEB_KIT.md` (إن وصلك مرفقًا فانسخه إلى هذا المسار؛ وإن وصلك نصًا فاكتبه إليه كما هو)، ثم شغّل من جذر المستودع:

```bash
python3 - docs/handoff/QAREEB_KIT.md <<'PY'
import pathlib, re, sys
src = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
marker = re.compile(r'^<!-- ===== FILE: (.+?) ===== -->\\n(.*?)^<!-- ===== END FILE ===== -->$', re.S | re.M)
count = 0
for path, body in marker.findall(src):
    target = pathlib.Path(path)
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_text(body, encoding='utf-8')
    count += 1
print(count, 'files written')
PY
```

يجب أن يطبع `{count} files written`. الناتج: `CLAUDE.md` في الجذر، والحقيبة في `docs/handoff/`.

## الخطوة ٢: ما ليس في هذه الحزمة

- `docs/handoff/04c-catalogue-texts.md`: نصوص الآيات والأحاديث في القائمة المرشحة، منسوخة من مصادرها. لا تكتبها؛ ولّدها بالسكريبت بعد تنزيل المصادر:

```bash
mkdir -p .sources/quran .sources/hadith
curl -fsS -o .sources/quran/uthmani.txt "https://tanzil.net/pub/download/index.php?marks=true&sajdah=true&rub=true&tatweel=true&quranType=uthmani&outType=txt-2&agree=true"
curl -fsS -o .sources/quran/simple.txt "https://tanzil.net/pub/download/index.php?marks=true&sajdah=true&rub=true&tatweel=true&quranType=simple&outType=txt-2&agree=true"
for c in bukhari muslim abudawud tirmidhi nasai ibnmajah; do curl -fsS -o .sources/hadith/$c.json "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/fawazahmed0/hadith-api@1/editions/ara-$c.min.json"; done
python3 -I docs/handoff/tools/build_catalogue_texts.py .sources > docs/handoff/04c-catalogue-texts.md
python3 -I docs/handoff/tools/verify_catalogue.py .sources | grep -c "✓"   # expect 112
echo ".sources/" >> .gitignore
```

- `docs/handoff/assets/script-compare.png`: صورة مقارنة الخطوط. موجودة في المستودع `adel3li/Alhamd-design-v2` تحت `handoff/dua-app/assets/`، وعند صاحب المشروع.

## الخطوة ٣: ابدأ

اقرأ `CLAUDE.md`، ثم `docs/handoff/00-decisions.md` (المحسوم والمفتوح)، ثم افتح `docs/handoff/08-kickoff-prompt.md` ونفّذ البرومبت المكتوب بين الخطين فيه كأنه رسالة صاحب المشروع إليك. خطوات «قبل اللصق» في أوله تمت بفكّ هذه الحزمة.

القاعدة الأولى قبل كل شيء: **النص المقدس يُنسخ من مصدره ولا يُكتب.** لا تكتب نص آية أو حديث من ذاكرتك في أي ملف أو رسالة.

---

'''


def files():
    rel = [KIT / name for name in ORDER]
    for sub in ['templates', 'reusable-code', 'tools']:
        rel += sorted(p for p in (KIT / sub).rglob('*') if p.is_file() and '__pycache__' not in p.parts)
    seen = set()
    for path in rel:
        r = path.relative_to(KIT).as_posix()
        if r in SKIP or r in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(r)
        yield f'docs/handoff/{r}', path
    yield 'CLAUDE.md', KIT / 'templates' / 'CLAUDE.md'


def main(out_path):
    entries = list(files())
    parts = [HEADER.replace('{count}', str(len(entries)))]
    for target, source in entries:
        body = source.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        for line in body.split('\n'):
            if line.startswith('<!-- ===== '):
                raise SystemExit(f'{source} contains a marker line; the pack would not split cleanly')
        if not body.endswith('\n'):
            raise SystemExit(f'{source} does not end with a newline; the pack would change it')
        parts.append(OPEN.format(target) + '\n' + body + CLOSE + '\n\n')
    pathlib.Path(out_path).write_text(''.join(parts), encoding='utf-8')
    print(len(entries), 'files packed into', out_path)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv[1])
<!-- ===== END FILE ===== -->

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/handoff/tools/verify_catalogue.py ===== -->
"""Reference check for handoff/dua-app/04-seed-catalogue.md.

Checks that every Qur'anic reference exists in Tanzil's text and that every
hadith number exists in the open hadith-api dataset (sunnah.com numbering;
Muslim by Abd al-Baqi number = the integer part of `arabicnumber`), and lists
the gradings recorded there, and applies the current scope (al-Bukhari and Muslim only;
pass --rule-b to see option B instead). Prints a Markdown report.

This script contains no Qur'an or hadith text and must not: it never types a
verse or a hadith. Whether the dua is really at a reference is checked when a
card is built, by comparing the card's text (pasted or fetched from the
source) with the source itself.

Usage: python3 -I verify_catalogue.py DOWNLOADS_DIR, where DOWNLOADS_DIR has
quran/simple.txt (Tanzil, txt-2 format) and hadith/<collection>.json
(hadith-api editions/ara-<collection>.min.json).
"""
import json, os, sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

D = sys.argv[1]
SCOPE = 'ruleB' if '--rule-b' in sys.argv[2:] else 'sahihayn'

quran = set()
for line in open(f'{D}/quran/simple.txt', encoding='utf-8'):
    parts = line.rstrip('\n').split('|')
    if len(parts) == 3 and parts[0].isdigit():
        quran.add((int(parts[0]), int(parts[1])))

from catalogue_refs import Q, S  # noqa: E402

H = {c: json.load(open(f'{D}/hadith/{c}.json'))['hadiths']
     for c in ['bukhari', 'muslim', 'abudawud', 'tirmidhi', 'nasai', 'ibnmajah']}

def find(coll, num):
    if coll == 'muslim':
        return [h for h in H[coll] if str(h.get('arabicnumber', '')).split('.')[0] == str(num)]
    return [h for h in H[coll] if h.get('hadithnumber') == num]

CN = {'bukhari': 'البخاري', 'muslim': 'مسلم', 'abudawud': 'أبو داود', 'tirmidhi': 'الترمذي', 'nasai': 'النسائي', 'ibnmajah': 'ابن ماجه'}
GRADERS = {'Al-Albani': 'الألباني', 'Muhammad Muhyi Al-Din Abdul Hamid': 'محمد محيي الدين عبد الحميد',
           'Shuaib Al Arnaut': 'شعيب الأرنؤوط', 'Zubair Ali Zai': 'زبير علي زئي', 'Ahmad Muhammad Shakir': 'أحمد شاكر',
           'Bashar Awad Maarouf': 'بشار عواد معروف', 'Abu Ghuddah': 'أبو غدة', 'Muhammad Fouad Abd al-Baqi': 'محمد فؤاد عبد الباقي'}
GRADES = {'Isnaad Sahih': 'إسناده صحيح', 'Isnaad Hasan': 'إسناده حسن', 'Hasan Isnaad': 'إسناده حسن',
          'Hasan Sahih': 'حسن صحيح', 'Sahih': 'صحيح', 'Hasan': 'حسن', 'Daif': 'ضعيف'}


# Option B of 00-decisions.md §5, mirrored from reusable-code/hadithGrade.ts.
import re
WEAK = re.compile(r'\b(daif|da\'if|mawdu|maudu|munkar|shadh|batil)\b', re.I)
NOT_MARFU = re.compile(r'\b(muquf|mauquf|mawquf|maqtu|mursal)\b', re.I)
ACCEPTED = re.compile(r'\b(sahih|hasan)\b', re.I)
DISSENT = ['Shuaib Al Arnaut', 'Ahmad Muhammad Shakir', 'Bashar Awad Maarouf']

def classify(grade):
    if WEAK.search(grade): return 'weak'
    if NOT_MARFU.search(grade): return 'not_marfu'
    if ACCEPTED.search(grade): return 'accepted'
    return 'unknown'

def rule_b(coll, gradings):
    if coll in ('bukhari', 'muslim'): return 'يدخل', 'في الصحيحين'
    if SCOPE == 'sahihayn': return 'مؤجل', 'خارج الصحيحين'
    primary = next((g for g in gradings if g['name'] == 'Al-Albani'), None)
    if not primary: return 'للمراجع', 'لا حكم للألباني في المصدر'
    c = classify(primary['grade'])
    if c == 'weak': return 'لا يدخل', 'ضعّفه الألباني'
    if c != 'accepted': return 'للمراجع', 'حكم الألباني يحتاج نظرًا'
    dissent = [GRADERS.get(g['name'], g['name']) for g in gradings if g['name'] in DISSENT and classify(g['grade']) == 'weak']
    if dissent: return 'للمراجع', 'صححه الألباني وضعّفه ' + '، '.join(dissent)
    if any(g['name'] in DISSENT and classify(g['grade']) == 'not_marfu' for g in gradings): return 'للمراجع', 'جعله أحد المحققين موقوفًا أو مقطوعًا'
    return 'يدخل', 'صححه أو حسّنه الألباني ولم يضعّفه محقق من القاعدة'

print('## أ. القرآن\n\n| # | الموضع | الآيات موجودة |\n|---|---|---|')
q_bad = 0
for row, s, a, b in Q:
    ok = all((s, i) in quran for i in range(a, b + 1))
    q_bad += not ok
    print(f'| {row} | {s}:{a}' + (f'–{b}' if b != a else '') + f' | {"✓" if ok else "✗"} |')
print(f'\n{len(Q)} موضعًا، {len(Q) - q_bad} موجودة.\n')

print('## ب. السنة\n\n| # | المرجع | الرقم موجود | الأحكام المسجّلة في المصدر | القرار الآن | السبب |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|')
for row, refs in S:
    for coll, num in refs:
        if num is None:
            print(f'| {row} | {coll} | — | ليس في المصدر المفتوح | مؤجل | خارج الصحيحين |')
            continue
        hs = find(coll, num)
        grades = '، '.join(f"{GRADERS.get(g['name'], g['name'])}: {GRADES.get(g['grade'], g['grade'])}"
                          for g in (hs[0].get('grades', []) if hs else []))
        if not grades and coll in ('bukhari', 'muslim'):
            grades = 'في الصحيح'
        decision, why = rule_b(coll, hs[0].get('grades', []) if hs else [])
        if any('\ufffd' in h['text'] for h in hs):
            why += '؛ وفي نص المصدر حرف تالف، فيُنقل من مصدر ثانٍ'
        print(f'| {row} | {CN[coll]} {num} | {"✓" if hs else "✗"} | {grades} | {decision} | {why} |')
<!-- ===== END FILE ===== -->

<!-- ===== FILE: CLAUDE.md ===== -->
# قريب (Qareeb)

Arabic-only, right-to-left, mobile-first web app: one supplication (dua) a day from the Qur'an and the Sunnah, with the situation it was said in, its meaning, short reflections and a five-step guided experience. Sister app of Alhamd (one blessing a day). React 19, TypeScript strict, Vite, Tailwind v4, Zustand. No backend: all user data is in `localStorage`. Installable PWA that works offline.

## Read before working

- `docs/handoff/README.md` indexes the starting kit: product brief, UX, content model, technical blueprint, lessons from Alhamd, roadmap.
- `docs/handoff/05-technical-blueprint.md` is the architecture. `docs/handoff/03-content-model.md` is the content model and the review process.
- `docs/reference/` documents the code as built. `storage.md` lists every `localStorage` key. Update it in the same pull request as the code.
- `docs/decisions.md` records the owner's decisions. Open decisions are listed at the end of `docs/handoff/01-product-brief.md`; do not take them yourself.

## Commands

```bash
bun install
bun run dev               # app at http://localhost:5173/
bun run lint              # tsc --noEmit, strict
bun run test              # vitest
bun run e2e -- --trace off
bun run validate-content  # schema, sacred-text and review checks on content/duas
bun run check-promises    # product promises as code checks
bun run build             # production build: content at CONTENT_MIN_LEVEL or above (verified, by the owner's decision)
CONTENT_PREVIEW=1 bun run build   # preview build: drafts included, marked
```

## Rules that are easy to break

- **Sacred text is copied, never written.** Qur'an text is copied by `scripts/fetch-quran.ts` from the source file in `content/sources/`, by surah and ayah. Hadith text is copied by `scripts/fetch-hadith.ts` from the hadith dataset, by collection and number, or pasted by a researcher from the source named in `hadith.sourceUrl`, with the gradings sunnah.com records; the current scope is al-Bukhari and Muslim only (`decide()` in `src/lib/hadithGrade.ts` defers every other collection; option B is ready there for when the scope widens). For now sunnah.com is the only source for `verified`; a match against Dorar (`review.checks.secondSourceMatched`) is required for `approved`. A narration with a corrupted character is not shown until it is copied from a second source. Both are shown to users exactly as copied. Never type, complete, correct, re-vowel or paraphrase a verse or a hadith from memory, in any file or reply; refer to one by its reference instead. A character a source has corrupted is never repaired by hand: copy that text from a second source. Tests use neutral Arabic text.
- **Three content layers.** Sacred text (verbatim), transmitted information (with a citation), authored text (reviewed). Authored text adds no event, name, quote or ruling that is not in the first two layers. Anything quoted inside authored text between ﴿ ﴾ or « » must match its source; `validate-content` checks the Qur'an fragments.
- **Review gate.** Two stages: `verified` (source verification by Claude, with a report in `review.verification`) then `approved` (the named scholarly reviewer, in `review.approval`). Production shows cards at `CONTENT_MIN_LEVEL` or above (`verified`, by the owner's decision, until a reviewer exists). Never set `approval` or the `approved` status yourself, and never write in the interface that the content was reviewed by scholars unless every card shown is `approved`.
- **Dates.** Every day key comes from `src/lib/dates.ts` (`toLocalDateString`, `parseLocalDateString`, `useLocalDay`). Never `toISOString().slice(0, 10)` or `new Date("YYYY-MM-DD")`: those are UTC, which is still yesterday until 03:00 in Riyadh.
- **Store what was shown.** A completion stores the dua id with the local day. Never recompute which dua a past day had from its date.
- **Storage keys.** One registry, `src/state/keys.ts`, one pattern `qareeb:name:v1`. Never rename a key or change a stored shape without a migration, a test for it and an update to `docs/reference/storage.md`. Nothing is written on first run except what the user did.
- **Product promises.** No streaks, consecutive-day counts, points, badges or percentages of days. No account. No network request from `src/` (the allow-list in `scripts/check-promises.ts` is empty). No user text in anything shared or exported except the user's own backup file. Do not show a setting or claim a feature that does not work.
- **Theming.** Components use semantic tokens from `src/styles/tokens.css` through Tailwind's `@theme`. No hex, `rgb(` or named colours in components. The theme is set on `<html>` only.
- **Overlays.** Every dialog renders through the shared `Dialog` component (portal to `document.body`, `useDialog` for focus, Escape and scroll lock). Never put `transform`, `filter` or `animation-fill-mode: forwards` on a screen root: a transformed ancestor becomes the containing block of `position: fixed` children.
- **Sacred text display.** Only through `SacredText`. Qur'an text is the King Fahd Complex text (`text_qpc_hafs` from api.quran.com, endpoint `/api/v4/quran/verses/qpc_hafs`, kept in `content/sources/`) in the Madina Mushaf font KFGQPC Uthmanic Script HAFS, self-hosted, in the app and on share images, so it reads like the printed mushaf. Do not pair that font with Tanzil's text. It is never truncated, in the app or on share images: shrink the type or grow the card.
- **Accessibility.** No `user-scalable=no` or `maximum-scale`. Honour `prefers-reduced-motion`. Paced text lives in a polite live region and can always be shown in full.
- **No developer tools in production builds.** Use `import.meta.env.DEV`.
- **Size.** Keep components under about 300 lines; pure logic goes in `src/lib/` with tests.

## Layout

`src/app/` shell, hash routing, error boundary. `src/screens/` one folder per screen. `src/features/` live-dua flow, share card, backup. `src/components/` shared UI (`Dialog`, `SacredText`…). `src/lib/` pure logic. `src/state/` store, keys, migrations. `src/content/` types, schema and the generated content. `src/copy/ar.ts` all interface text. `content/` the dua cards and their sources. `scripts/` content and promise checks. `landing/` the static marketing page.

## Verifying a change

Type check, unit tests and the content and promise checks must pass. For UI, check in a browser at 390×844 and 360×640, in light and dark, and with a large text size, before saying it works.
<!-- ===== END FILE ===== -->
